====================================================================================================
QUESTION: If the insured dies accidentally during Year 2, what does the beneficiary receive — graded benefit or full benefit?
CATEGORY: CAT4_MULTIHOP_REASONING
EXPECTED ANSWER: The full benefit amount ($10,000) plus all premiums paid, because accidental death at any time triggers the full benefit regardless of the graded benefit schedule.
EVIDENCE: During Year 2 (May 1, 2025 - April 30, 2026): We pay 70% of face amount ($7,000) plus all premiums paid / EXCEPTION - ACCIDENTAL DEATH: If death results from an accident at any time, we pay the full benefit amount immediately, plus all premiums paid.
====================================================================================================

--- GOAL PLAN ---
rewritten_query: What is the death benefit payout for accidental death in Year 2 under a typical life insurance policy with a graded benefit structure?
reasoning: The query asks about the payout amount (graded benefit vs. full benefit) under a specific condition: if the insured dies accidentally during Year 2 of a policy. This is a policy-specific benefit question that depends on the terms of a life insurance policy, particularly one with a graded death benefit structure (common in certain types of policies like guaranteed issue or simplified issue life insurance). The key here is to determine whether the policy pays the full benefit or a reduced (graded) benefit when death occurs due to an accident in Year 2. This requires understanding the policy’s benefit schedule, specifically the payout rules for accidental death in the second year. Since the query does not name a specific policy, it likely refers to a general rule in such policies. The answer hinges on knowing the policy’s graded benefit structure, particularly the benefit level in Year 2 for accidental death.

"If death occurs during Year 1 (before May 1, 2025), the insurer pays 30% of the face amount." -> (has ) -> [policy year with graded death benefit (Graded death benefit phase)]

"If death occurs during Year 2 (May 1, 2025 - April 30, 2026), the insurer pays $7,000." -> LEADS_TO -> "If death occurs after Year 2 (on or after May 1, 2026), the insurer pays $10,000." -> (has ) -> [policy year with graded death benefit (Graded death benefit phase)]
Logical Conclusions:
After Year 2, the death benefit reaches full face value, indicating the completion of the graded benefit period.

"If death occurs after Year 2 (on or after May 1, 2026), the insurer pays $10,000." -> IS_QUANTIFIED_BY -> "If death occurs after Year 2 (on or after May 1, 2026), the insurer pays the full benefit amount."
Logical Conclusions:
After Year 2, the full death benefit of $10,000 is paid, which matches the face amount.

[policy year with graded death benefit (Graded death benefit phase)] -> (participates in event: ) -> "If death results from an accident at any time, the insurer pays the full benefit amount immediately."

--- GENERATION ---
LLM answer: The beneficiary receives the full benefit amount, not the graded benefit, because accidental death triggers the full payout regardless of the policy year.
